    explainSecurityBusinessRulesAndContext
    05.26.2026 12:06:02 pm 05.26.2026 12:06:02 pm 00:00:00:005 · #test-id=1
    FinanceInvoiceSecurityAPI CRUDSanity
    Overview: Security Verification & Boundary Context
    StatusTimestampDetails
    Info 12:06:02 pm Priority: 0
    Info 12:06:02 pm Description: Overview: Security Verification & Boundary Context
    Info 12:06:02 pm Start Time: Tue May 26 12:06:02 IST 2026
    Info 12:06:02 pm

    AP Non-PO Invoice Workflow Security Verification

    This test suite verifies the security and integrity of the AP Non-PO Invoice Approval Workflow. Specifically, it checks if user role boundaries are correctly enforced and if premature or unauthorized approvals are rejected. The business requirements for approval delegation (Delegation of Authority - DOA thresholds) are as follows:

    Invoice Amount Approval Levels Required Designated Approver (Role) User Email
    ≤ 10,000 SAR None (Auto-Approved) - -
    > 10,000 SAR to ≤ 100,000 SAR L1 + L2 L1: Senior Accountant
    L2: Finance Controller    		 khushbu.patel@visionwaves.com
    abinaya.saravanan@visionwaves.com
    > 100,000 SAR (e.g. 120,000 SAR tested) L1 + L2 + L3 L1: Senior Accountant
    L2: Finance Controller
    L3: Sr. Group Accounting Manager    		 khushbu.patel@visionwaves.com
    abinaya.saravanan@visionwaves.com
    Suyash.tiwari@visionwaves.com

    Why We Perform This Security Activity:

    • Enforce Role Isolation (SoD): Verify that users cannot sign off or patch Camunda tasks belonging to other approval levels (e.g., L1 cannot approve L2 tasks; L3 cannot approve L1 tasks).
    • Prevent Premature Approvals: Test if higher-level approvers can submit approvals out of sequence. Prematurely calling the /post-ap-invoice endpoint must be blocked to prevent bypassing previous stages.
    • Validate Invalid Credentials: Ensure that random users, non-authorized roles (like the Accountant who created the invoice), or completely invalid/forged JWT tokens are rejected by the APIs.
    • Identify and Log Vulnerabilities: Document cases where the API returns 200 OK instead of HTTP error codes (e.g. >= 400) for security violations, indicating where backend verification logic needs strengthening.
    Pass 12:06:02 pm Test Passed: explainSecurityBusinessRulesAndContext
